PCLake	FUNCTION SMPULSE(mVar, x, i, nm, tw)	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       Call: e.g. uVar = SMPULSE(mVar, Time, iPulse, nmVarMax, PulseWidth)	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       Smooth pulse, starting at the stated time and top at 0.5*tw later.	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       Uses the data in the TABLE array mVar: 1 to n = values,	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C          n+1 to 2n = times.	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       nm = max. number of data values, tw = width of pulse.	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       The count (i) should be initialized in the model.	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLake	$	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C     (Dollar sign means: model variables are accessible in this function.)	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	INTEGER i, nm, Size	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	DOUBLEPRECISION mVar(2*nm), x, tw	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLake	IF(x .LT. mVar(nm+i)) THEN	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	SMPULSE = 0.0	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	ELSE IF(x .LE. mVar(nm+i)+tw) THEN	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	SMPULSE = 1.0/tw * (COS(2*Pi*(x-mVar(nm+i)-0.5*tw)/tw) + 1.0)	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	&   * mVar(i)	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	ELSE	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	SMPULSE = 0.0	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	i = i + 1	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	ENDIF	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLake	RETURN	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	END	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLake	C***********************************************************************	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	DOUBLEPRECISION FUNCTION WindFunc(WindSpeed,FetchLength,	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	&                                  WaterDepth,SedRoughness)	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	DOUBLEPRECISION WindSpeed,FetchLength,WaterDepth,	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	&                SedRoughness	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	DOUBLEPRECISION Grav, Pi, RhoW, F_s, D_s, H_s, T_s	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	DOUBLEPRECISION H, T, L_0, L, a_lm, U_bg, f_wg	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       TauWind  : shear stress by wind action	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	Grav  =    9.8	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	Pi    =    3.141592	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	RhoW  = 1000.0	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C--------------------------------CHECK for WindSpeed > 0	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	IF ( WindSpeed .LT. 0.0001 ) THEN	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	WindFunc = 0.0D0	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	RETURN	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	ENDIF	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C--------------------------------TauWind calculation:	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	F_s = Grav*FetchLength/(WindSpeed**2)	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	D_s = Grav*WaterDepth/(WindSpeed**2)	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       WRITE(6,*)'F_s = ',F_s,' D_s = ', D_s	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	H_s = 0.24 * DTANH( 0.710 * D_s ** 0.763 ) * DTANH(	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	&        ( 0.015 * F_s ** 0.45 ) / DTANH( 0.710 * D_s ** 0.763 ))	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	T_s = 2.0 * Pi * DTANH( 0.855 * D_s ** 0.365 ) * DTANH(	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	&        ( 0.0345 * F_s ** 0.37 ) / DTANH( 0.855 * D_s ** 0.365 ))	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       WRITE(6,*)'H_s = ',H_s,' T_s = ', T_s	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	H = ( H_s * WindSpeed**2 ) / Grav	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	T = ( T_s * WindSpeed ) / Grav	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C        WRITE(6,*)'H = ',H,' T = ', T	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	L_0 = ( Grav * T**2 ) / ( 2.0 * Pi )	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	L   = L_0 * DSQRT( DTANH( ( 2.0 * Pi * WaterDepth ) / L_0 ))	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       WRITE(6,*)'L_0 = ',L_0,' L = ', L	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	U_bg = ( Pi * H ) /	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	&                  ( T * DSINH( (2.0 * Pi * WaterDepth) / L ) )	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	a_lm = ( U_bg * T ) / ( 2.0 * Pi )	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       WRITE(6,*)'U_bg = ',U_bg,' a_lm = ', a_lm	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	f_wg = 0.16 * DSQRT( SedRoughness / a_lm )	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	WindFunc = f_wg * 0.5 * RhoW * U_bg**2	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C       WRITE(6,*)'f_wg = ',f_wg,' TauWind = ', TauWind	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C--------------------------------RETURN	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	RETURN	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	END	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
PCLake	C----------------------------------------------------------------------	42	Extra function	Not in Excel	Not in Excel		42						
		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42

PCLTran	! Seasonal nutrient loading and water inflow.	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLTran	!   Note: calculated only if UseSeasonLoad = True. If used, around April 1 and	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLTran	!   Oct 1, inflow and loading values change gradually (sinusoid function)	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLTran	!   in (default) 10 days from winter to summer values or opposite.	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42

PCLTran	!  For substances: surface outflow ("Outfl") = dilution ("Dil")	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLTran	!      minus infiltration ("InfW")	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42

PCLTran	wNTranPhyt = wNTranDiat + wNTranGren + wNTranBlue	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! total algal transport flux [gN/m3/d]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! Silica:	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	uSiLoadSiO2  = cSiO2In * uQIn / mmPerm  ! SiO2 input [gSi/m2/d]	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! Note: is calculated from constant Si concentration in input water.	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	uSiLoadDet   = cSiDDetIn * uDLoadDet	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	uSiLoadDiat  = cSiDDiat * uDLoadDiat	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	uSiLoad = uSiLoadSiO2 + uSiLoadDet + uSiLoadDiat	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wSiDilSiO2 = ukDil * sSiO2W          ! [gSi/m3/d]	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wSiDilDet  = ukDil * sSiDetW ! [gSi/m3/d]	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wSiDilDiat = cSiDDiat * wDDilDiat     ! dilution [gSi/m3/d]	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wSiOutflTot = ukOut * (sSiO2W + sSiDetW + cSiDDiat * sDDiatW)	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! total surface outflow [gSi/m3/d]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wSiTranSiO2 = uSiLoadSiO2 / sDepthW - wSiDilSiO2	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wSiTranDetW = uSiLoadDet / sDepthW - wSiDilDet	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	tSiTranTotT = uSiLoadSiO2 + uSiLoadDet + uSiLoadDiat  &	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	- (wSiDilDet + wSiDilSiO2 + wSiDilDiat) * sDepthW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42		IF (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	ELSE	42	ELSE	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! Dilution:	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wPDilPhyt = ukDil * sPPhytW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! dilution of Phyt [gP/m3/d]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wNDilPhyt = ukDil * sNPhytW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! dilution of Gren [gN/m3/d]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wDDilPhyt = ukDil * sDPhytW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! dilution of Blue [g/m3/d]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! Total transport fluxes of algae:	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wDTranPhyt = uDLoadPhyt / sDepthW - wDDilPhyt	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wPTranPhyt = uPLoadPhyt / sDepthW - wPDilPhyt	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wNTranPhyt = uNLoadPhyt / sDepthW - wNDilPhyt	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	! Silica:	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wSiTranSiO2 = 0.0	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN				
PCLTran	wSiTranDetW = 0.0	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42		IFNOT (InclThreeAlg) THEN

PCLTran	ENDIF	42	ENDIF	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	-1	42

PCLAbio	tO2MinDetS   = molO2molC * cCPerDW * tDMinOxyDetS	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42

PCLAbio		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLAbio	tDDenitS = oNO3S**2.0 / (hNO3Denit**2.0 + oNO3S**2.0)  &	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLAbio	* (1.0 - afOxySed) * (1.0 - fRefrDetS) * tDMinDetS	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLAbio	!mineralisation flux by denitrification [gD.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42

PCLAbio	aPAdsMaxW = cRelPAdsD + aCorO2BOD * cRelPAdsFe * fFeDIM  &	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLAbio	+ cRelPAdsAl * fAlDIM	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42

PCLAbio	+ cRelPAdsAl * fAlDIM	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42

PCLAbio	!P adsorption affinity, corrected for redox conditions [m3/gP]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42

PCLBed	! Assimilation and egestion by herbivorous birds:	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed	! Note: assim. efficiency (fDAssBird) assumed equal for D,P and N.	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLBed	tDAssVegBird = fDAssBird * tDGrazVegBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	tDEgesBird   = tDGrazVegBird - tDAssVegBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLBed	tPAssVegBird  = fDAssBird * tPGrazVegBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	tPEgesBird    = tPGrazVegBird - tPAssVegBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	tPEgesBirdPO4 = fDissEgesBird * tPEgesBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	tPEgesBirdDet = tPEgesBird - tPEgesBirdPO4	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLBed	tNAssVegBird  = fDAssBird * tNGrazVegBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	tNEgesBird    = tNGrazVegBird - tNAssVegBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	tNEgesBirdNH4 = fDissEgesBird * tNEgesBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	tNEgesBirdDet = tNEgesBird - tNEgesBirdNH4	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42

PCLBed	! FLUXES to/from Vegetation module to other components:	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLBed	wDBedDetW = (tDMortVegW  + tDEgesBird) / sDepthW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total DW flux from Vegetation module to water detritus [gD.m-3.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed	tDBedDetS = tDMortVegS	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total DW flux from Vegetation module to sediment detritus [gD.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed	tDBedTotT = tDMigrVeg + tDProdVeg - tDRespVeg - tDManVeg - tDAssVegBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total DW flux from Vegetation module [gD.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLBed	wPBedPO4W = (- tPUptVegW + tPExcrVegW + tPMortVegPO4W + tPEgesBirdPO4) /sDepthW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total P flux from Vegetation module to PO4 in water [gP.m-3.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed	wPBedDetW = (tPMortVegDetW + tPEgesBirdDet) / sDepthW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total P flux from Vegetation module to water detritus [gP.m-3.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42

PCLBed	! total P flux from Vegetation module to pore water PO4 [gP.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed	tPBedDetS = tPMortVegDetS	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total P flux from Vegetation module to sediment detritus [gP.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed	tPBedTotT = tPMigrVeg - tPManVeg - tPAssVegBird	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total P flux from Vegetation module [gP.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed		42	Blank	Blank	Blank		42						
PCLBed	wNBedNH4W = (- tNUptNH4VegW + tNExcrVegW + tNMortVegNH4W + tNEgesBirdNH4) &	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	/ sDepthW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total N flux from Vegetation module to NH4 in water [gN.m-3.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed	wNBedNO3W =  - tNUptNO3VegW / sDepthW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42

PCLBed	wNBedDetW = (tNMortVegDetW + tNEgesBirdDet) / sDepthW	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42

PCLBed	! total N flux from Vegetation module to NO3 in pore water [gN.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42						
PCLBed	tNBedDetS =  tNMortVegDetS	42	AUXIL	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)	Derivatives(NOT Derivatives)		42						
PCLBed	! total N flux from Vegetation module to sediment detritus [gN.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42

PCLBed	! total N flux from Vegetation module [gN.m-2.d-1]	42	Comment	Comment	Comment		42
